[76] user: <heartbeat>
  <automation_id>ai-ui</automation_id>
  <current_time_iso>2026-05-22T15:49:00.639Z</current_time_iso>
  <instructions>
围绕“AI 游戏平台：Demo 免费试玩，完整版付费下载；无链上资产；钱包仅作为可选登录/支付身份”的 Web 端产品，进行一轮极简且美观的 UI/功能设计优化。重点原则：平台功能必须简约，不做复杂游戏商店、开发者后台、链上资产、复杂下载管理或复杂筛选；UI 交互只围绕用户体验 Demo 和支付下载完整游戏。核心路径固定为：看到游戏 -> Play Demo -> 试玩 -> Buy Full Game -> 支付 -> Download。必须特别关注 UI 交互页面美观：试玩入口、购买按钮、支付状态、购买后 Download 状态、My Games 已购列表，都要有清晰层级、舒服留白、好看的游戏媒体展示、按钮状态一致、移动/桌面不拥挤。输出内容包括：1. 极简产品功能列表，只保留玩家端核心功能：游戏发现、免费试玩、登录、支付、下载、My Games 再次下载/更新；2. 用户使用流程，重点分析 Demo 试玩到付费购买的转化路径；3. 当前 UI 可删除/弱化的复杂功能，例如开发者分成面板、过多筛选项、复杂 checkout summary、钱包入口、下载管理复杂状态、平台后台感信息；4. 本轮 UI 优化方向：Play Demo 作为最强按钮，不登录也能试玩；Buy Full Game 固定可见并显示明确价格；支付成功后 Buy 变 Download；My Games 只显示已购游戏；交互页面要更美观、更清楚、更轻量；5. 使用 imagegen 输出该平台所有关键交互 UI 图，不只输出主页面。每轮至少生成这些界面图：A. 游戏发现/详情主页面；B. Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。
  </instructions>
</heartbeat>

围绕“AI 游戏平台：Demo 免费试玩，完整版付费下载；无链上资产；钱包仅作为可选登录/支付身份”的 Web 端产品，进行一轮极简且美观的 UI/功能设计优化。重点原则：平台功能必须简约，不做复杂游戏商店、开发者后台、链上资产、复杂下载管理或复杂筛选；UI 交互只围绕用户体验 Demo 和支付下载完整游戏。核心路径固定为：看到游戏 -> Play Demo -> 试玩 -> Buy Full Game -> 支付 -> Download。必须特别关注 UI 交互页面美观：试玩入口、购买按钮、支付状态、购买后 Download 状态、My Games 已购列表，都要有清晰层级、舒服留白、好看的游戏媒体展示、按钮状态一致、移动/桌面不拥挤。输出内容包括：1. 极简产品功能列表，只保留玩家端核心功能：游戏发现、免费试玩、登录、支付、下载、My Games 再次下载/更新；2. 用户使用流程，重点分析 Demo 试玩到付费购买的转化路径；3. 当前 UI 可删除/弱化的复杂功能，例如开发者分成面板、过多筛选项、复杂 checkout summary、钱包入口、下载管理复杂状态、平台后台感信息；4. 本轮 UI 优化方向：Play Demo 作为最强按钮，不登录也能试玩；Buy Full Game 固定可见并显示明确价格；支付成功后 Buy 变 Download；My Games 只显示已购游戏；交互页面要更美观、更清楚、更轻量；5. 使用 imagegen 输出该平台所有关键交互 UI 图，不只输出主页面。每轮至少生成这些界面图：A. 游戏发现/详情主页面；B. Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。
  </instructions>
</heartbeat>

围绕“AI 游戏平台：Demo 免费试玩，完整版付费下载；无链上资产；钱包仅作为可选登录/支付身份”的 Web 端产品，进行一轮极简且美观的 UI/功能设计优化。重点原则：平台功能必须简约，不做复杂游戏商店、开发者后台、链上资产、复杂下载管理或复杂筛选；UI 交互只围绕用户体验 Demo 和支付下载完整游戏。核心路径固定为：看到游戏 -> Play Demo -> 试玩 -> Buy Full Game -> 支付 -> Download。必须特别关注 UI 交互页面美观：试玩入口、购买按钮、支付状态、购买后 Download 状态、My Games 已购列表，都要有清晰层级、舒服留白、好看的游戏媒体展示、按钮状态一致、移动/桌面不拥挤。输出内容包括：1. 极简产品功能列表，只保留玩家端核心功能：游戏发现、免费试玩、登录、支付、下载、My Games 再次下载/更新；2. 用户使用流程，重点分析 Demo 试玩到付费购买的转化路径；3. 当前 UI 可删除/弱化的复杂功能，例如开发者分成面板、过多筛选项、复杂 checkout summary、钱包入口、下载管理复杂状态、平台后台感信息；4. 本轮 UI 优化方向：Play Demo 作为最强按钮，不登录也能试玩；Buy Full Game 固定可见并显示明确价格；支付成功后 Buy 变 Download；My Games 只显示已购游戏；交互页面要更美观、更清楚、更轻量；5. 使用 imagegen 输出该平台所有关键交互 UI 图，不只输出主页面。每轮至少生成这些界面图：A. 游戏发现/详情主页面；B. Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。
  </instructions>
</heartbeat>

Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。
  </instructions>
</heartbeat>

围绕“AI 游戏平台：Demo 免费试玩，完整版付费下载；无链上资产；钱包仅作为可选登录/支付身份”的 Web 端产品，进行一轮极简且美观的 UI/功能设计优化。重点原则：平台功能必须简约，不做复杂游戏商店、开发者后台、链上资产、复杂下载管理或复杂筛选；UI 交互只围绕用户体验 Demo 和支付下载完整游戏。核心路径固定为：看到游戏 -> Play Demo -> 试玩 -> Buy Full Game -> 支付 -> Download。必须特别关注 UI 交互页面美观：试玩入口、购买按钮、支付状态、购买后 Download 状态、My Games 已购列表，都要有清晰层级、舒服留白、好看的游戏媒体展示、按钮状态一致、移动/桌面不拥挤。输出内容包括：1. 极简产品功能列表，只保留玩家端核心功能：游戏发现、免费试玩、登录、支付、下载、My Games 再次下载/更新；2. 用户使用流程，重点分析 Demo 试玩到付费购买的转化路径；3. 当前 UI 可删除/弱化的复杂功能，例如开发者分成面板、过多筛选项、复杂 checkout summary、钱包入口、下载管理复杂状态、平台后台感信息；4. 本轮 UI 优化方向：Play Demo 作为最强按钮，不登录也能试玩；Buy Full Game 固定可见并显示明确价格；支付成功后 Buy 变 Download；My Games 只显示已购游戏；交互页面要更美观、更清楚、更轻量；5. 使用 imagegen 输出该平台所有关键交互 UI 图，不只输出主页面。每轮至少生成这些界面图：A. 游戏发现/详情主页面；B. Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。
  </instructions>
</heartbeat>

Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。
  </instructions>

围绕“AI 游戏平台：Demo 免费试玩，完整版付费下载；无链上资产；钱包仅作为可选登录/支付身份”的 Web 端产品，进行一轮极简且美观的 UI/功能设计优化。重点原则：平台功能必须简约，不做复杂游戏商店、开发者后台、链上资产、复杂下载管理或复杂筛选；UI 交互只围绕用户体验 Demo 和支付下载完整游戏。核心路径固定为：看到游戏 -> Play Demo -> 试玩 -> Buy Full Game -> 支付 -> Download。必须特别关注 UI 交互页面美观：试玩入口、购买按钮、支付状态、购买后 Download 状态、My Games 已购列表，都要有清晰层级、舒服留白、好看的游戏媒体展示、按钮状态一致、移动/桌面不拥挤。输出内容包括：1. 极简产品功能列表，只保留玩家端核心功能：游戏发现、免费试玩、登录、支付、下载、My Games 再次下载/更新；2. 用户使用流程，重点分析 Demo 试玩到付费购买的转化路径；3. 当前 UI 可删除/弱化的复杂功能，例如开发者分成面板、过多筛选项、复杂 checkout summary、钱包入口、下载管理复杂状态、平台后台感信息；4. 本轮 UI 优化方向：Play Demo 作为最强按钮，不登录也能试玩；Buy Full Game 固定可见并显示明确价格；支付成功后 Buy 变 Download；My Games 只显示已购游戏；交互页面要更美观、更清楚、更轻量；5. 使用 imagegen 输出该平台所有关键交互 UI 图，不只输出主页面。每轮至少生成这些界面图：A. 游戏发现/详情主页面；B. Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。
  </instructions>
</heartbeat>

Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。
  </instructions>

围绕“AI 游戏平台：Demo 免费试玩，完整版付费下载；无链上资产；钱包仅作为可选登录/支付身份”的 Web 端产品，进行一轮极简且美观的 UI/功能设计优化。重点原则：平台功能必须简约，不做复杂游戏商店、开发者后台、链上资产、复杂下载管理或复杂筛选；UI 交互只围绕用户体验 Demo 和支付下载完整游戏。核心路径固定为：看到游戏 -> Play Demo -> 试玩 -> Buy Full Game -> 支付 -> Download。必须特别关注 UI 交互页面美观：试玩入口、购买按钮、支付状态、购买后 Download 状态、My Games 已购列表，都要有清晰层级、舒服留白、好看的游戏媒体展示、按钮状态一致、移动/桌面不拥挤。输出内容包括：1. 极简产品功能列表，只保留玩家端核心功能：游戏发现、免费试玩、登录、支付、下载、My Games 再次下载/更新；2. 用户使用流程，重点分析 Demo 试玩到付费购买的转化路径；3. 当前 UI 可删除/弱化的复杂功能，例如开发者分成面板、过多筛选项、复杂 checkout summary、钱包入口、下载管理复杂状态、平台后台感信息；4. 本轮 UI 优化方向：Play Demo 作为最强按钮，不登录也能试玩；Buy Full Game 固定可见并显示明确价格；支付成功后 Buy 变 Download；My Games 只显示已购游戏；交互页面要更美观、更清楚、更轻量；5. 使用 imagegen 输出该平台所有关键交互 UI 图，不只输出主页面。每轮至少生成这些界面图：A. 游戏发现/详情主页面；B. Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。
  </instructions>
</heartbeat>

Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。
  </instructions>
</heartbeat>

func _add_rect_collision(parent: Node, position: Vector2, size: Vector2) -> void:
	var body := StaticBody2D.new()
	body.position = position
	body.collision_layer = LAYER_WORLD
	body.collision_mask = 0
	parent.add_child(body)

	var collision := CollisionShape2D.new()
	var shape := RectangleShape2D.new()
	shape.size = size
	collision.shape = shape
	body.add_child(collision)


func _create_grid(width: int, height: int) -> Array:
	var grid := []
	for _y in range(height):
		var row := []
		for _x in range(width):
			row.append(WALL)
		grid.append(row)
	return grid


func _carve_room(grid: Array, room: Rect2i) -> void:
	for y in range(room.position.y, room.end.y):
		for x in range(room.position.x, room.end.x):
			grid[y][x] = FLOOR


func _connect_rooms(grid: Array, from_room: Rect2i, to_room: Rect2i) -> void:
	var a := _room_center_cell(from_room)
	var b := _room_center_cell(to_room)
	for x in range(mini(a.x, b.x), maxi(a.x, b.x) + 1):
		_carve_corridor_cell(grid, Vector2i(x, a.y))
	for y in range(mini(a.y, b.y), maxi(a.y, b.y) + 1):
		_carve_corridor_cell(grid, Vector2i(b.x, y))


func _carve_corridor_cell(grid: Array, cell: Vector2i) -> void:
	for y in range(cell.y - 1, cell.y + 2):
		for x in range(cell.x - 1, cell.x + 2):
			if y >= 0 and y < grid.size() and x >= 0 and x < grid[y].size():
				grid[y][x] = FLOOR


func _add_tile(parent: Node, cell: Vector2i, atlas_cell: Vector2i) -> void:
	var sprite := Sprite2D.new()
	sprite.texture = dungeon_tileset
	sprite.region_enabled = true
	sprite.region_rect = _dungeon_rect(atlas_cell)
	sprite.scale = Vector2(2.0, 2.0)
	sprite.position = _cell_center(cell)
	parent.add_child(sprite)

func _on_mobile_bow_pressed() -> void:
	if finished or mobile_paused:
		return
	if codex_ai_enabled:
		message_label.text = "Assist warrior uses Attack."
	else:
		_start_player_attack("bow")


func _toggle_mobile_pause() -> void:
	if finished:
		return
	mobile_paused = not mobile_paused
	mobile_move_vector = Vector2.ZERO
	if mobile_pause_panel:
		mobile_pause_panel.visible = mobile_paused


func _on_collectible_body_entered(body: Node, area: Area2D) -> void:
	if not _can_body_open_collectible(body) or finished:
		return
	if bool(area.get_meta("opened")):
		return

	var kind := String(area.get_meta("kind"))
	var amount := int(area.get_meta("amount"))
	var chest_grade := String(area.get_meta("chest_grade", "common"))
	area.set_meta("opened", true)
	_notify_slime_chest_opened(area)
	_notify_chapter_monster_chest_opened(area)
	var sprite := area.get_node_or_null("ChestSprite") as Sprite2D
	if sprite:
		sprite.texture = _open_chest_texture()
	var glow := area.get_node_or_null("ChestGlow") as Sprite2D
	if glow:
		glow.visible = false
	var collision := area.get_node_or_null("ChestCollision") as CollisionShape2D
	if collision:
		collision.set_deferred("disabled", true)

	if kind == "gem":
		collected_gems += amount
		message_label.text = "Opened a chest: +%d gems." % amount
	else:
		collected_coins += amount
		wallet_coins += amount
		_save_monster_companion_state()
		message_label.text = "Opened a chest: +%d coins." % amount
	_show_reward_popup(area.global_position, kind, amount)
	_apply_maze_chest_reward(area)
	var treasure_id := _roll_chapter_treasure_drop(chest_grade)
	if not treasure_id.is_empty():
		var is_new := _register_treasure_drop(treasure_id)
		var treasure_def := _treasure_def(treasure_id)
		var treasure_name := String(treasure_def.get("name", treasure_id))
		if is_new:
			message_label.text += " New treasure: %s." % treasure_name
		else:
			message_label.text += " Duplicate treasure: %s." % treasure_name
		_show_treasure_reward_popup(area.global_position, treasure_id, is_new)
		_refresh_collection_panel()
	_refresh_bag_panel()
	_update_hud()


func _apply_maze_chest_reward(area: Area2D) -> void:
	if current_chapter != CHAPTER_SLIME:
		return
	var role := String(area.get_meta("maze_role", ""))
	if role == "exit_key":
		maze_keys = mini(MAZE_REQUIRED_KEYS, maze_keys + 1)
		var bonus := 10 if _maze_has_relic("lucky_key") else 0
		if bonus > 0:
			collected_coins += bonus
			wallet_coins += bonus
			_save_monster_companion_state()
		message_label.text += " Exit key found%s." % (" +%d lucky coins" % bonus if bonus > 0 else "")
		_show_status_popup(area.global_position + Vector2(0.0, -42.0), "Exit Key", Color(0.36, 1.0, 0.9))
	elif role == "relic" or role == "core":
		var relic := _roll_maze_relic()
		if not relic.is_empty():
			maze_relics.append(relic)
			if maze_relics.size() > MAZE_MAX_RELICS:
				maze_relics.pop_front()
			message_label.text += " Relic: %s." % _maze_relic_name(relic)
			_show_status_popup(area.global_position + Vector2(0.0, -42.0), _maze_relic_name(relic), Color(0.52, 1.0, 0.80))
		if role == "core":
			maze_risk += 1
	maze_risk += 0 if _maze_has_relic("silent_boots") else 1

func _register_treasure_drop(treasure_id: String) -> bool:
	var is_new := not collected_treasures.has(treasure_id)
	collected_treasures[treasure_id] = true
	treasure_counts[treasure_id] = int(treasure_counts.get(treasure_id, 0)) + 1
	_save_collection_state()
	return is_new


func _can_body_open_collectible(body: Node) -> bool:
	if body == player:
		return true
	return codex_ai_enabled and body == assist_player


func _closed_chest_texture() -> Texture2D:
	if current_chapter == CHAPTER_SLIME:
		var texture := _load_png_texture("res://assets/generated/maze_treasure/maze_chest_common.png")
		if texture:
			return texture
	if current_chapter == CHAPTER_FOREST and forest_chest_closed:
		return forest_chest_closed
	return chest_closed


func _open_chest_texture() -> Texture2D:
	if current_chapter == CHAPTER_SLIME:
		var texture := _load_png_texture("res://assets/generated/maze_treasure/maze_chest_relic.png")
		if texture:
			return texture
	if current_chapter == CHAPTER_FOREST and forest_chest_open:
		return forest_chest_open
	return chest_open

+func _chest_sprite_scale(texture: Texture2D) -> Vector2:
+	if texture == null:
+		return Vector2.ONE
+	var size := texture.get_size()
+	if size.x <= 0.0 or size.y <= 0.0:
+		return Vector2.ONE
+	var scale_factor: float = minf(CHEST_VISUAL_TARGET_SIZE.x / size.x, CHEST_VISUAL_TARGET_SIZE.y / size.y)
+	return Vector2.ONE * scale_factor
+

+	match maze_role:

  <current_time_iso>2026-05-22T15:49:00.639Z</current_time_iso>
  <instructions>
围绕“AI 游戏平台：Demo 免费试玩，完整版付费下载；无链上资产；钱包仅作为可选登录/支付身份”的 Web 端产品，进行一轮极简且美观的 UI/功能设计优化。重点原则：平台功能必须简约，不做复杂游戏商店、开发者后台、链上资产、复杂下载管理或复杂筛选；UI 交互只围绕用户体验 Demo 和支付下载完整游戏。核心路径固定为：看到游戏 -> Play Demo -> 试玩 -> Buy Full Game -> 支付 -> Download。必须特别关注 UI 交互页面美观：试玩入口、购买按钮、支付状态、购买后 Download 状态、My Games 已购列表，都要有清晰层级、舒服留白、好看的游戏媒体展示、按钮状态一致、移动/桌面不拥挤。输出内容包括：1. 极简产品功能列表，只保留玩家端核心功能：游戏发现、免费试玩、登录、支付、下载、My Games 再次下载/更新；2. 用户使用流程，重点分析 Demo 试玩到付费购买的转化路径；3. 当前 UI 可删除/弱化的复杂功能，例如开发者分成面板、过多筛选项、复杂 checkout summary、钱包入口、下载管理复杂状态、平台后台感信息；4. 本轮 UI 优化方向：Play Demo 作为最强按钮，不登录也能试玩；Buy Full Game 固定可见并显示明确价格；支付成功后 Buy 变 Download；My Games 只显示已购游戏；交互页面要更美观、更清楚、更轻量；5. 使用 imagegen 输出该平台所有关键交互 UI 图，不只输出主页面。每轮至少生成这些界面图：A. 游戏发现/详情主页面；B. Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。
  </instructions>
</heartbeat>

func _add_rect_collision(parent: Node, position: Vector2, size: Vector2) -> void:
	var body := StaticBody2D.new()
	body.position = position
	body.collision_layer = LAYER_WORLD
	body.collision_mask = 0
	parent.add_child(body)

	var collision := CollisionShape2D.new()
	var shape := RectangleShape2D.new()
	shape.size = size
	collision.shape = shape
	body.add_child(collision)


func _create_grid(width: int, height: int) -> Array:
	var grid := []
	for _y in range(height):
		var row := []
		for _x in range(width):
			row.append(WALL)
		grid.append(row)
	return grid


func _carve_room(grid: Array, room: Rect2i) -> void:
	for y in range(room.position.y, room.end.y):
		for x in range(room.position.x, room.end.x):
			grid[y][x] = FLOOR


func _connect_rooms(grid: Array, from_room: Rect2i, to_room: Rect2i) -> void:
	var a := _room_center_cell(from_room)
	var b := _room_center_cell(to_room)
	for x in range(mini(a.x, b.x), maxi(a.x, b.x) + 1):
		_carve_corridor_cell(grid, Vector2i(x, a.y))
	for y in range(mini(a.y, b.y), maxi(a.y, b.y) + 1):
		_carve_corridor_cell(grid, Vector2i(b.x, y))


func _carve_corridor_cell(grid: Array, cell: Vector2i) -> void:
	for y in range(cell.y - 1, cell.y + 2):
		for x in range(cell.x - 1, cell.x + 2):
			if y >= 0 and y < grid.size() and x >= 0 and x < grid[y].size():
				grid[y][x] = FLOOR


func _add_tile(parent: Node, cell: Vector2i, atlas_cell: Vector2i) -> void:
	var sprite := Sprite2D.new()
	sprite.texture = dungeon_tileset
	sprite.region_enabled = true
	sprite.region_rect = _dungeon_rect(atlas_cell)
	sprite.scale = Vector2(2.0, 2.0)
	sprite.position = _cell_center(cell)
	parent.add_child(sprite)

func _on_mobile_bow_pressed() -> void:
	if finished or mobile_paused:
		return
	if codex_ai_enabled:
		message_label.text = "Assist warrior uses Attack."
	else:
		_start_player_attack("bow")


func _toggle_mobile_pause() -> void:
	if finished:
		return
	mobile_paused = not mobile_paused
	mobile_move_vector = Vector2.ZERO
	if mobile_pause_panel:
		mobile_pause_panel.visible = mobile_paused


func _on_collectible_body_entered(body: Node, area: Area2D) -> void:
	if not _can_body_open_collectible(body) or finished:
		return
	if bool(area.get_meta("opened")):
		return

	var kind := String(area.get_meta("kind"))
	var amount := int(area.get_meta("amount"))
	var chest_grade := String(area.get_meta("chest_grade", "common"))
	area.set_meta("opened", true)
	_notify_slime_chest_opened(area)
	_notify_chapter_monster_chest_opened(area)
	var sprite := area.get_node_or_null("ChestSprite") as Sprite2D
	if sprite:
		sprite.texture = _open_chest_texture()
	var glow := area.get_node_or_null("ChestGlow") as Sprite2D
	if glow:
		glow.visible = false
	var collision := area.get_node_or_null("ChestCollision") as CollisionShape2D
	if collision:
		collision.set_deferred("disabled", true)

	if kind == "gem":
		collected_gems += amount
		message_label.text = "Opened a chest: +%d gems." % amount
	else:
		collected_coins += amount
		wallet_coins += amount
		_save_monster_companion_state()
		message_label.text = "Opened a chest: +%d coins." % amount
	_show_reward_popup(area.global_position, kind, amount)
	_apply_maze_chest_reward(area)
	var treasure_id := _roll_chapter_treasure_drop(chest_grade)
	if not treasure_id.is_empty():
		var is_new := _register_treasure_drop(treasure_id)
		var treasure_def := _treasure_def(treasure_id)
		var treasure_name := String(treasure_def.get("name", treasure_id))
		if is_new:
			message_label.text += " New treasure: %s." % treasure_name
		else:
			message_label.text += " Duplicate treasure: %s." % treasure_name
		_show_treasure_reward_popup(area.global_position, treasure_id, is_new)
		_refresh_collection_panel()
	_refresh_bag_panel()
	_update_hud()


func _apply_maze_chest_reward(area: Area2D) -> void:
	if current_chapter != CHAPTER_SLIME:
		return
	var role := String(area.get_meta("maze_role", ""))
	if role == "exit_key":
		maze_keys = mini(MAZE_REQUIRED_KEYS, maze_keys + 1)
		var bonus := 10 if _maze_has_relic("lucky_key") else 0
		if bonus > 0:
			collected_coins += bonus
			wallet_coins += bonus
			_save_monster_companion_state()
		message_label.text += " Exit key found%s." % (" +%d lucky coins" % bonus if bonus > 0 else "")
		_show_status_popup(area.global_position + Vector2(0.0, -42.0), "Exit Key", Color(0.36, 1.0, 0.9))
	elif role == "relic" or role == "core":
		var relic := _roll_maze_relic()
		if not relic.is_empty():
			maze_relics.append(relic)
			if maze_relics.size() > MAZE_MAX_RELICS:
				maze_relics.pop_front()
			message_label.text += " Relic: %s." % _maze_relic_name(relic)
			_show_status_popup(area.global_position + Vector2(0.0, -42.0), _maze_relic_name(relic), Color(0.52, 1.0, 0.80))
		if role == "core":
			maze_risk += 1
	maze_risk += 0 if _maze_has_relic("silent_boots") else 1

func _register_treasure_drop(treasure_id: String) -> bool:
	var is_new := not collected_treasures.has(treasure_id)
	collected_treasures[treasure_id] = true
	treasure_counts[treasure_id] = int(treasure_counts.get(treasure_id, 0)) + 1
	_save_collection_state()
	return is_new


func _can_body_open_collectible(body: Node) -> bool:
	if body == player:
		return true
	return codex_ai_enabled and body == assist_player


func _closed_chest_texture() -> Texture2D:
	if current_chapter == CHAPTER_SLIME:
		var texture := _load_png_texture("res://assets/generated/maze_treasure/maze_chest_common.png")
		if texture:
			return texture
	if current_chapter == CHAPTER_FOREST and forest_chest_closed:
		return forest_chest_closed
	return chest_closed


func _open_chest_texture() -> Texture2D:
	if current_chapter == CHAPTER_SLIME:
		var texture := _load_png_texture("res://assets/generated/maze_treasure/maze_chest_relic.png")
		if texture:
			return texture
	if current_chapter == CHAPTER_FOREST and forest_chest_open:
		return forest_chest_open
	return chest_open

+func _chest_sprite_scale(texture: Texture2D) -> Vector2:
+	if texture == null:
+		return Vector2.ONE
+	var size := texture.get_size()
+	if size.x <= 0.0 or size.y <= 0.0:
+		return Vector2.ONE
+	var scale_factor: float = minf(CHEST_VISUAL_TARGET_SIZE.x / size.x, CHEST_VISUAL_TARGET_SIZE.y / size.y)
+	return Vector2.ONE * scale_factor

[16] user: <heartbeat>
  <automation_id>ai-ui</automation_id>
  <current_time_iso>2026-05-22T15:49:00.639Z</current_time_iso>
  <instructions>
围绕“AI 游戏平台：Demo 免费试玩，完整版付费下载；无链上资产；钱包仅作为可选登录/支付身份”的 Web 端产品，进行一轮极简且美观的 UI/功能设计优化。重点原则：平台功能必须简约，不做复杂游戏商店、开发者后台、链上资产、复杂下载管理或复杂筛选；UI 交互只围绕用户体验 Demo 和支付下载完整游戏。核心路径固定为：看到游戏 -> Play Demo -> 试玩 -> Buy Full Game -> 支付 -> Download。必须特别关注 UI 交互页面美观：试玩入口、购买按钮、支付状态、购买后 Download 状态、My Games 已购列表，都要有清晰层级、舒服留白、好看的游戏媒体展示、按钮状态一致、移动/桌面不拥挤。输出内容包括：1. 极简产品功能列表，只保留玩家端核心功能：游戏发现、免费试玩、登录、支付、下载、My Games 再次下载/更新；2. 用户使用流程，重点分析 Demo 试玩到付费购买的转化路径；3. 当前 UI 可删除/弱化的复杂功能，例如开发者分成面板、过多筛选项、复杂 checkout summary、钱包入口、下载管理复杂状态、平台后台感信息；4. 本轮 UI 优化方向：Play Demo 作为最强按钮，不登录也能试玩；Buy Full Game 固定可见并显示明确价格；支付成功后 Buy 变 Download；My Games 只显示已购游戏；交互页面要更美观、更清楚、更轻量；5. 使用 imagegen 输出该平台所有关键交互 UI 图，不只输出主页面。每轮至少生成这些界面图：A. 游戏发现/详情主页面；B. Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。
  </instructions>
</heartbeat>

围绕“AI 游戏平台：Demo 免费试玩，完整版付费下载；无链上资产；钱包仅作为可选登录/支付身份”的 Web 端产品，进行一轮极简且美观的 UI/功能设计优化。重点原则：平台功能必须简约，不做复杂游戏商店、开发者后台、链上资产、复杂下载管理或复杂筛选；UI 交互只围绕用户体验 Demo 和支付下载完整游戏。核心路径固定为：看到游戏 -> Play Demo -> 试玩 -> Buy Full Game -> 支付 -> Download。必须特别关注 UI 交互页面美观：试玩入口、购买按钮、支付状态、购买后 Download 状态、My Games 已购列表，都要有清晰层级、舒服留白、好看的游戏媒体展示、按钮状态一致、移动/桌面不拥挤。输出内容包括：1. 极简产品功能列表，只保留玩家端核心功能：游戏发现、免费试玩、登录、支付、下载、My Games 再次下载/更新；2. 用户使用流程，重点分析 Demo 试玩到付费购买的转化路径；3. 当前 UI 可删除/弱化的复杂功能，例如开发者分成面板、过多筛选项、复杂 checkout summary、钱包入口、下载管理复杂状态、平台后台感信息；4. 本轮 UI 优化方向：Play Demo 作为最强按钮，不登录也能试玩；Buy Full Game 固定可见并显示明确价格；支付成功后 Buy 变 Download；My Games 只显示已购游戏；交互页面要更美观、更清楚、更轻量；5. 使用 imagegen 输出该平台所有关键交互 UI 图，不只输出主页面。每轮至少生成这些界面图：A. 游戏发现/详情主页面；B. Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。

[16] user: <heartbeat>
  <automation_id>ai-ui</automation_id>
  <current_time_iso>2026-05-22T15:49:00.639Z</current_time_iso>
  <instructions>
围绕“AI 游戏平台：Demo 免费试玩，完整版付费下载；无链上资产；钱包仅作为可选登录/支付身份”的 Web 端产品，进行一轮极简且美观的 UI/功能设计优化。重点原则：平台功能必须简约，不做复杂游戏商店、开发者后台、链上资产、复杂下载管理或复杂筛选；UI 交互只围绕用户体验 Demo 和支付下载完整游戏。核心路径固定为：看到游戏 -> Play Demo -> 试玩 -> Buy Full Game -> 支付 -> Download。必须特别关注 UI 交互页面美观：试玩入口、购买按钮、支付状态、购买后 Download 状态、My Games 已购列表，都要有清晰层级、舒服留白、好看的游戏媒体展示、按钮状态一致、移动/桌面不拥挤。输出内容包括：1. 极简产品功能列表，只保留玩家端核心功能：游戏发现、免费试玩、登录、支付、下载、My Games 再次下载/更新；2. 用户使用流程，重点分析 Demo 试玩到付费购买的转化路径；3. 当前 UI 可删除/弱化的复杂功能，例如开发者分成面板、过多筛选项、复杂 checkout summary、钱包入口、下载管理复杂状态、平台后台感信息；4. 本轮 UI 优化方向：Play Demo 作为最强按钮，不登录也能试玩；Buy Full Game 固定可见并显示明确价格；支付成功后 Buy 变 Download；My Games 只显示已购游戏；交互页面要更美观、更清楚、更轻量；5. 使用 imagegen 输出该平台所有关键交互 UI 图，不只输出主页面。每轮至少生成这些界面图：A. 游戏发现/详情主页面；B. Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。
  </instructions>
</heartbeat>

围绕“AI 游戏平台：Demo 免费试玩，完整版付费下载；无链上资产；钱包仅作为可选登录/支付身份”的 Web 端产品，进行一轮极简且美观的 UI/功能设计优化。重点原则：平台功能必须简约，不做复杂游戏商店、开发者后台、链上资产、复杂下载管理或复杂筛选；UI 交互只围绕用户体验 Demo 和支付下载完整游戏。核心路径固定为：看到游戏 -> Play Demo -> 试玩 -> Buy Full Game -> 支付 -> Download。必须特别关注 UI 交互页面美观：试玩入口、购买按钮、支付状态、购买后 Download 状态、My Games 已购列表，都要有清晰层级、舒服留白、好看的游戏媒体展示、按钮状态一致、移动/桌面不拥挤。输出内容包括：1. 极简产品功能列表，只保留玩家端核心功能：游戏发现、免费试玩、登录、支付、下载、My Games 再次下载/更新；2. 用户使用流程，重点分析 Demo 试玩到付费购买的转化路径；3. 当前 UI 可删除/弱化的复杂功能，例如开发者分成面板、过多筛选项、复杂 checkout summary、钱包入口、下载管理复杂状态、平台后台感信息；4. 本轮 UI 优化方向：Play Demo 作为最强按钮，不登录也能试玩；Buy Full Game 固定可见并显示明确价格；支付成功后 Buy 变 Download；My Games 只显示已购游戏；交互页面要更美观、更清楚、更轻量；5. 使用 imagegen 输出该平台所有关键交互 UI 图，不只输出主页面。每轮至少生成这些界面图：A. 游戏发现/详情主页面；B. Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。
  </instructions>
</heartbeat>

.demo-panel {
  min-height: 620px;
}

.progress-meta {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 14px;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.progress-meta strong {
  color: var(--green);
}

.bar {
  height: 8px;
  margin: 12px 0 24px;
  overflow: hidden;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);
}

.bar span,
.bar i {
  display: block;
  height: 100%;
  border-radius: inherit;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--cyan), #62d1ff);
}

.controls {
  grid-column: 1;
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 16px 28px;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 18px 22px;
  box-shadow: none;
}

kbd {
  padding: 6px 9px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line-strong);
  border-radius: 6px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  color: var(--text);
}

.controls p {
  flex-basis: 100%;
  margin: 2px 0 0;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.checkout-shell {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(270px, 360px) minmax(0, 620px);
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 22px;
  align-items: start;
}

.summary-card,
.checkout-card {
  padding: 24px;
}

.summary-art {
  min-height: 190px;
  height: 190px;
  margin: 16px 0 20px;
  border-radius: 8px;
}

.summary-card h2 {
  font-size: 28px;
}

dl {
  display: grid;
  gap: 14px;
  margin: 0;
}

dl div {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 16px;
  padding-bottom: 12px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
}

dt {
  color: var(--soft);
}

dd {
  margin: 0;
  color: var(--text);
}

.checkout-card h1 {
  font-size: 44px;
}

form {
  display: grid;
  <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="412" />ent {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  margin-top: 18px;
  padding: 14px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 8px;
  color: var(--muted);
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.035);
}

.success-shell {
  display: grid;
  gap: 24px;
}

.success-title {
  text-align: center;
}

.success-title span {
  display: inline-flex;
  width: 50px;
  height: 50px;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  border-radius: 50%;
  color: var(--green);
  border: 1px solid rgba(92, 224, 139, 0.42);
  background: rgba(92, 224, 139, 0.08);
}

.success-title h1 {
  margin: 14px 0 8px;
}

.success-title p {
  color: var(--muted);
}

.segmented {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 8px;
  margin: 8px 0 18px;
}

.segmented button {
  min-height: 44px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 8px;
  color: var(--muted);
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04);
}

.segmented button.active {
  color: var(--text);
  border-color: rgba(24, 167, 255, 0.48);
  background: rgba(24, 167, 255, 0.14);
}

.details {
  display: grid;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 0;
  margin: 20px 0;
  list-style: none;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.details li::before {
  content: "";
  display: inline-block;
  width: 6px;
  height: 6px;
  margin-right: 10px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--cyan);
  vertical-align: middle;
}

.library-shell {
  display: grid;
  gap: 18px;
}

.library-head {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 18px;
  align-items: end;
}

.library-head h1 {
  margin-bottom: 8px;
}

.library-head p {
  color: var(--muted);
}

.library-head aside {
  min-width: 250px;
  padding: 18px;
  display: grid;
  gap: 6px;
  box-shadow: none;
}

.library-head aside span {
  color: var(--muted);
}

.library-list {
  display: grid;
  gap: 14px;
}

.library-row {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 104px minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
  gap: 16px;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 14px;
  box-shadow: none;

.library-main {
  min-width: 0;
}

.library-main > div {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 10px;
  align-items: center;
}

.owned {
  padding: 4px 8px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(92, 224, 139, 0.36);
  border-radius: 999px;
  color: var(--green);
  font-size: 13px;
}

.compact {
  min-width: 140px;
  min-height: 46px;
  padding: 0 16px;
}

.download-progress {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: auto 1fr auto;
  gap: 12px;
  align-items: center;
  margin-top: 12px;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.download-progress .bar {
  margin: 0;
}

.download-progress button {
  border: 0;
  color: var(--muted);
  background: transparent;
}

.browse-demos {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 16px;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 18px;
  box-shadow: none;
}

.browse-demos p {
  margin: 0;
  color: var(--muted);
}

@media (max-width: 980px) {
  .topbar {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr auto;
  }

  .nav,
  .search {
    grid-row: 2;
  }

  .search {
    grid-column: 1 / -1;
  }

  .detail-grid,
  .download-grid,
  .demo-shell,
  .checkout-shell {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .media-card,
  .player-card {
    min-height: 430px;
  }

  .demo-frame {
    height: 520px;
  }

  .demo-panel {
    min-height: auto;
  }

  .controls {
    grid-column: auto;
  }

  .related {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .library-head {
    align-items: stretch;
    flex-direction: column;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 640px) {
  .shell,
  .demo-shell,
  .checkout-shell,
  .success-shell,
  .library-shell {
    width: min(100vw - 28px, 1480px);
    margin-top: 18px;
  }

  .topbar {
    padding: 14px;
    gap: 12px;
  }

  .brand span {
    font-size: 19px;
  }

  .ghost span {
    display: none;
  }

  .action-panel,
  .summary-card,
  .checkout-card {
    padding: 20px;
  }

  .action-panel h1,
  h1 {
    font-size: 34px;
  }

  .flow {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .flow i {
    display: none;
  }

  .game-card,
  .library-row {
    grid-template-columns: 86px 1fr;
  }

  .game-card .mini,
  .library-row .compact {
    grid-column: 1 / -1;
    width: 100%;
  }

  .form-row {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .download-progress {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .browse-demos {
    align-items: stretch;
    flex-direction: column;
  }
}

import { createServer } from "node:http";
import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { extname, join, normalize } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const root = fileURLToPath(new URL(".", import.meta.url));
const port = Number(process.env.PORT || 5180);
const rpcUrl = process.env.SOLANA_RPC_URL || "https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com";

const config = {
  gameId: "clockwork",
  gameTitle: "Quiet Dungeon Treasure",
  amountUsdt: "8",
  amountBaseUnits: 8_000_000n,
  tokenDecimals: 6,
  tokenSymbol: "USDT",
  chain: "Solana Mainnet",
  recipient: "7MsAkdANC9d9MxRz1G1nndz1F8TnHnWsZBFp2EVQiYE9",
  usdtMint: "Es9vMFrzaCERmJfrF4H2FYD4KCoNkY11McCe8BenwNYB",
};

const orders = new Map();
const mimeTypes = {
  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
  ".js": "text/javascript; charset=utf-8",
  ".css": "text/css; charset=utf-8",
  ".png": "image/png",
  ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
  ".jpeg": "image/jpeg",
  ".wasm": "application/wasm",
  ".pck": "application/octet-stream",
  ".zip": "application/zip",
};

const alphabet = "123456789ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijkmnopqrstuvwxyz";

function base58(bytes) {
  let value = BigInt(`0x${Buffer.from(bytes).toString("hex")}`);
  let encoded = "";
  while (value > 0n) {
    const mod = value % 58n;
    encoded = alphabet[Number(mod)] + encoded;
    value /= 58n;
  }
  for (const byte of bytes) {
    if (byte === 0) encoded = "1" + encoded;
    else break;
  }
  return encoded || "1";
}

function json(res, status, payload) {
  const data = JSON.stringify(payload);
  res.writeHead(status, {
    "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
    "cache-control": "no-store",
  });
  res.end(data);
}

async function body(req) {
  const chunks = [];
  for await (const chunk of req) chunks.push(chunk);
  if (!chunks.length) return {};
  return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8"));
}

function shortId() {
  return base58(randomBytes(9));
}

function publicOrder(order) {
  return {
    id: order.id,
    gameId: order.gameId,
    walletAddress: order.walletAddress,
    amount: config.amountUsdt,
    token: config.tokenSymbol,
    chain: config.chain,
    recipient: config.recipient,
    usdtMint: config.usdtMint,
    reference: order.reference,
    memo: order.memo,
    status: order.status,
    signature: order.signature || "",
    explorerUrl: order.signature ? `https://solscan.io/tx/${order.signature}` : "",
    solanaPayUrl: order.solanaPayUrl,
    expiresAt: order.expiresAt,
    error: order.error || "",
  };
}

function createOrder(walletAddress, gameId) {
  const id = `qdt_${Date.now()}_${shortId()}`;
  const reference = base58(randomBytes(32));
  const memo = `AIGAME:${id}`;
  const params = new URLSearchParams({
    amount: config.amountUsdt,
    "spl-token": config.usdtMint,
    reference,
    label: "AIGame Dock",
    message: `Unlock ${config.gameTitle}`,
    memo,
  });
  const order = {
    id,
    gameId,
    walletAddress,
    reference,
    memo,
    status: "waiting_payment",
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 20 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
    solanaPayUrl: `solana:${config.recipient}?${params.toString()}`,
  };
  orders.set(id, order);
  return order;
}

async function rpc(method, params) {
  const response = await fetch(rpcUrl, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id: "aigame-dock", method, params }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Solana RPC ${response.status}`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (payload.error) throw new Error(payload.error.message || "Solana RPC error");
  return payload.result;

import { createServer } from "node:http";
import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { extname, join, normalize } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const root = fileURLToPath(new URL(".", import.meta.url));
const port = Number(process.env.PORT || 5180);
const rpcUrl = process.env.SOLANA_RPC_URL || "https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com";

const config = {
  gameId: "clockwork",
  gameTitle: "Quiet Dungeon Treasure",
  amountUsdt: "8",
  amountBaseUnits: 8_000_000n,
  tokenDecimals: 6,
  tokenSymbol: "USDT",
  chain: "Solana Mainnet",
  recipient: "7MsAkdANC9d9MxRz1G1nndz1F8TnHnWsZBFp2EVQiYE9",
  usdtMint: "Es9vMFrzaCERmJfrF4H2FYD4KCoNkY11McCe8BenwNYB",
};

const orders = new Map();
const mimeTypes = {
  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
  ".js": "text/javascript; charset=utf-8",
  ".css": "text/css; charset=utf-8",
  ".png": "image/png",
  ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
  ".jpeg": "image/jpeg",
  ".wasm": "application/wasm",
  ".pck": "application/octet-stream",
  ".zip": "application/zip",
};

const alphabet = "123456789ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijkmnopqrstuvwxyz";

function base58(bytes) {
  let value = BigInt(`0x${Buffer.from(bytes).toString("hex")}`);
  let encoded = "";
  while (value > 0n) {
    const mod = value % 58n;
    encoded = alphabet[Number(mod)] + encoded;
    value /= 58n;
  }
  for (const byte of bytes) {
    if (byte === 0) encoded = "1" + encoded;
    else break;
  }
  return encoded || "1";
}

function json(res, status, payload) {
  const data = JSON.stringify(payload);
  res.writeHead(status, {
    "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
    "cache-control": "no-store",
  });
  res.end(data);
}

async function body(req) {
  const chunks = [];
  for await (const chunk of req) chunks.push(chunk);
  if (!chunks.length) return {};
  return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8"));
}

function shortId() {
  return base58(randomBytes(9));
}

function publicOrder(order) {
  return {
    id: order.id,
    gameId: order.gameId,
    walletAddress: order.walletAddress,
    amount: config.amountUsdt,
    token: config.tokenSymbol,
    chain: config.chain,
    recipient: config.recipient,
    usdtMint: config.usdtMint,
    reference: order.reference,
    memo: order.memo,
    status: order.status,
    signature: order.signature || "",
    explorerUrl: order.signature ? `https://solscan.io/tx/${order.signature}` : "",
    solanaPayUrl: order.solanaPayUrl,
    expiresAt: order.expiresAt,
    error: order.error || "",
  };
}

function createOrder(walletAddress, gameId) {
  const id = `qdt_${Date.now()}_${shortId()}`;
  const reference = base58(randomBytes(32));
  const memo = `AIGAME:${id}`;
  const params = new URLSearchParams({
    amount: config.amountUsdt,
    "spl-token": config.usdtMint,
    reference,
    label: "AIGame Dock",
    message: `Unlock ${config.gameTitle}`,
    memo,
  });
  const order = {
    id,
    gameId,
    walletAddress,
    reference,
    memo,
    status: "waiting_payment",
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 20 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
    solanaPayUrl: `solana:${config.recipient}?${params.toString()}`,
  };
  orders.set(id, order);
  return order;
}

async function rpc(method, params) {
  const response = await fetch(rpcUrl, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: "2.0", id: "aigame-dock", method, params }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Solana RPC ${response.status}`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (payload.error) throw new Error(payload.error.message || "Solana RPC error");
  return payload.result;

async function buildUsdtPaymentTransaction(order) {
  const web3 = await import(solanaWeb3SdkUrl);
  const splToken = await import(solanaSplTokenSdkUrl);
  const connection = new web3.Connection(solanaRpcUrl, "confirmed");
  const payer = new web3.PublicKey(state.walletAddress);
  const recipient = new web3.PublicKey(order.recipient);
  const mint = new web3.PublicKey(order.usdtMint);
  const sourceAta = await splToken.getAssociatedTokenAddress(mint, payer);
  const recipientAta = await splToken.getAssociatedTokenAddress(mint, recipient);
  const amount = BigInt(Math.round(Number(order.amount) * 10 ** 6));
  const tx = new web3.Transaction();

  const createAtaInstruction =
    splToken.createAssociatedTokenAccountIdempotentInstruction ||
    splToken.createAssociatedTokenAccountInstruction;
  tx.add(createAtaInstruction(payer, recipientAta, recipient, mint));
  tx.add(
    splToken.createTransferCheckedInstruction(
      sourceAta,
      mint,
      recipientAta,
      payer,
      amount,
      6,
    ),
  );
  tx.add(
    new web3.TransactionInstruction({
      programId: new web3.PublicKey(memoProgramId),
      keys: [{ pubkey: new web3.PublicKey(order.reference), isSigner: false, isWritable: false }],
      data: new TextEncoder().encode(order.memo),
    }),
  );

  const latest = await connection.getLatestBlockhash("confirmed");
  tx.feePayer = payer;
  tx.recentBlockhash = latest.blockhash;
  return { tx, connection };
}

async function sendWithWalletStandard(tx) {

}

async function payWithWallet() {
  if (!state.walletAddress) {
    state.walletError = "Connect a Solana wallet before payment.";
    setView("wallet");
    return;
  }
  if (!state.walletSigner) {
    state.paymentError = "Reconnect MetaMask Solana before payment signing.";
    render();
    return;
  }
  if (!state.order) {
    const order = await createPaymentOrder();
    if (!order) return;
  }
  state.paymentBusy = true;
  state.paymentError = "";
  state.paymentNotice = "Preparing USDT transfer. Your wallet will ask you to confirm.";
  render();
  try {
    const { tx, connection } = await buildUsdtPaymentTransaction(state.order);
    let signature = await sendWithWalletStandard(tx);
    if (!signature) signature = await sendWithInjectedWallet(tx, connection);
    if (!signature) throw new Error("Connected wallet does not support transaction signing.");
    state.order.signature = signature;
    state.paymentNotice = `Payment submitted: ${shortAddress(signature)}. Verifying on Solana...`;
    render();
    const paid = await verifyPaymentSignature(signature);
    if (paid) return;
  } catch (error) {
    state.paymentError = error && error.message ? error.message : "Wallet payment failed.";
  }
  state.paymentBusy = false;
  render();
}

async function pay() {
  await payWithWallet();
}

async function buildUsdtPaymentTransaction(order) {
  const web3 = await import(solanaWeb3SdkUrl);
  const splToken = await import(solanaSplTokenSdkUrl);
  const connection = new web3.Connection(solanaRpcUrl, "confirmed");
  const payer = new web3.PublicKey(state.walletAddress);
  const recipient = new web3.PublicKey(order.recipient);
  const mint = new web3.PublicKey(order.usdtMint);
  const sourceAta = await splToken.getAssociatedTokenAddress(mint, payer);
  const recipientAta = await splToken.getAssociatedTokenAddress(mint, recipient);
  const amount = BigInt(Math.round(Number(order.amount) * 10 ** 6));
  const tx = new web3.Transaction();

  const createAtaInstruction =
    splToken.createAssociatedTokenAccountIdempotentInstruction ||
    splToken.createAssociatedTokenAccountInstruction;
  tx.add(createAtaInstruction(payer, recipientAta, recipient, mint));
  tx.add(
    splToken.createTransferCheckedInstruction(
      sourceAta,
      mint,
      recipientAta,
      payer,
      amount,
      6,
    ),
  );
  tx.add(
    new web3.TransactionInstruction({
      programId: new web3.PublicKey(memoProgramId),
      keys: [{ pubkey: new web3.PublicKey(order.reference), isSigner: false, isWritable: false }],
      data: new TextEncoder().encode(order.memo),
    }),
  );

  const latest = await connection.getLatestBlockhash("confirmed");
  tx.feePayer = payer;
  tx.recentBlockhash = latest.blockhash;
  return { tx, connection };
}

async function sendWithWalletStandard(tx) {
  const feature = state.walletSigner?.features?.["solana:signAndSendTransaction"];
  if (!feature || typeof featu<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="332" />r.status === "paid") {
    setPaid(payload.order);
    return true;
  }
  state.paymentError = payload.order.error || "Payment submitted but not confirmed yet. Try Verify Payment again.";
  return false;
}

async function payWithWallet() {
  if (!state.walletAddress) {
    state.walletError = "Connect a Solana wallet before payment.";
    setView("wallet");
    return;
  }
  if (!state.walletSigner) {
    state.paymentError = "Reconnect MetaMask Solana before payment signing.";
    render();
    return;
  }
  if (!state.order) {
    const order = await createPaymentOrder();
    if (!order) return;
  }
  state.paymentBusy = true;
  state.paymentError = "";
  state.paymentNotice = "Preparing USDT transfer. Your wallet will ask you to confirm.";
  render();
  try {
    const { tx, connection } = await buildUsdtPaymentTransaction(state.order);
    let signature = await sendWithWalletStandard(tx);
    if (!signature) signature = await sendWithInjectedWallet(tx, connection);
    if (!signature) throw new Error("Connected wallet does not support transaction signing.");
    state.order.signature = signature;
    state.paymentNotice = `Payment submitted: ${shortAddress(signature)}. Verifying on Solana...`;
    render();
    const paid = await verifyPaymentSignature(signature);
    if (paid) return;
  } catch (error) {
    state.paymentError = error && error.message ? error.message : "Wallet payment failed.";
  }
  state.paymentBusy = false;
  render();
}

async function pay() {
  await payWithWallet();
}

async function verifyPayment() {
  if (!state.order) return;
  state.paymentBusy = true;
  state.paymentError = "";
  state.paymentNotice = "";

围绕“AI 游戏平台：Demo 免费试玩，完整版付费下载；无链上资产；钱包仅作为可选登录/支付身份”的 Web 端产品，进行一轮极简且美观的 UI/功能设计优化。重点原则：平台功能必须简约，不做复杂游戏商店、开发者后台、链上资产、复杂下载管理或复杂筛选；UI 交互只围绕用户体验 Demo 和支付下载完整游戏。核心路径固定为：看到游戏 -> Play Demo -> 试玩 -> Buy Full Game -> 支付 -> Download。必须特别关注 UI 交互页面美观：试玩入口、购买按钮、支付状态、购买后 Download 状态、My Games 已购列表，都要有清晰层级、舒服留白、好看的游戏媒体展示、按钮状态一致、移动/桌面不拥挤。输出内容包括：1. 极简产品功能列表，只保留玩家端核心功能：游戏发现、免费试玩、登录、支付、下载、My Games 再次下载/更新；2. 用户使用流程，重点分析 Demo 试玩到付费购买的转化路径；3. 当前 UI 可删除/弱化的复杂功能，例如开发者分成面板、过多筛选项、复杂 checkout summary、钱包入口、下载管理复杂状态、平台后台感信息；4. 本轮 UI 优化方向：Play Demo 作为最强按钮，不登录也能试玩；Buy Full Game 固定可见并显示明确价格；支付成功后 Buy 变 Download；My Games 只显示已购游戏；交互页面要更美观、更清楚、更轻量；5. 使用 imagegen 输出该平台所有关键交互 UI 图，不只输出主页面。每轮至少生成这些界面图：A. 游戏发现/详情主页面；B. Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。
  </instructions>
</heartbeat>

async function sendWithWalletStandard(tx) {
  const feature = state.walletSigner?.features?.["solana:signAndSendTransaction"];
  if (!feature || typeof feature.signAndSendTransaction !== "function" || !state.walletAccount) {
    return "";
  }
  const txBytes = tx.serialize({ requireAllSignatures: false, verifySignatures: false });
  const result = await feature.signAndSendTransaction({
    account: state.walletAccount,
    chain: solanaChain,
    transaction: txBytes,
  });
  const signature = result?.signature || result?.signatures?.[0];
  return signature instanceof Uint8Array ? base58(signature) : signature || "";
}

async function sendWithInjectedWallet(tx, connection) {
  const provider = state.walletSigner;
  if (!provider) return "";
  if (typeof provider.signAndSendTransaction === "function") {
    const result = await provider.signAndSendTransaction(tx);
    return result?.signature?.toString?.() || result?.signature || "";
  }
  if (typeof provider.signTransaction === "function") {
    const signed = await provider.signTransaction(tx);
    const signature = await connection.sendRawTransaction(signed.serialize(), {
      skipPreflight: false,
      preflightCommitment: "confirmed",
    });
    return signature;
  }
  return "";
}

    const paid = await verifyPaymentSignature(signature);
    if (paid) return;
  } catch (error) {
    state.paymentError = error && error.message ? error.message : "Wallet payment failed.";
  }
  state.paymentBusy = false;
  render();
}

function download() {

function shortAddress(address) {
  if (!address) return "";
  return `${address.slice(0, 6)}...${address.slice(-4)}`;
}

function setConnectedWallet(address, provider, signer = null, account = null) {
  state.walletAddress = address;
  state.walletProvider = provider;
  state.walletSigner = signer;
  state.walletAccount = account;
  state.walletError = "";
  localStorage.setItem("aigameDockWallet", state.walletAddress);
  localStorage.setItem("aigameDockWalletProvider", state.walletProvider);
  setView("detail");
}

async function connectMetaMaskSolana() {
  const module = await import(metaMaskSolanaSdkUrl);
  const createSolanaClient = module.createSolanaClient || module.default?.createSolanaClient;
  if (!createSolanaClient) {
    throw new Error("MetaMask Solana connector did not load.");
  }

  const client = await createSolanaClient({
    dapp: {
      name: brandName,
      url: window.location.href,
    },
    api: {
      supportedNetworks: {
        mainnet: solanaRpcUrl,
      },
    },
  });
  const wallet = typeof client.getWallet === "function" ? client.getWallet() : client.wallet;
  const connectFeature = wallet?.features?.["standard:connect"];
  if (!connectFeature || typeof connectFeature.connect !== "function") {
    throw new Error("MetaMask Solana account is not available.");
  }

  const result = await connectFeature.connect({ silent: false });
  const account = result?.accounts?.[0] || wallet.accounts?.[0];
  const address = account?.address || account?.publicKey?.toString();
  if (!address) {
    throw new Error("No Solana account returned from MetaMask.");
  }
  return { address, wallet, account };
}

async function connectInjectedSolanaWallet() {
  const providers = [
    window.solana,
    window.phantom?.solana,
    window.backpack?.solana,
    window.solflare,
  ].filter(Boolean);

  for (const provider of providers) {
    if (typeof provider.connect !== "function") continue;
    const result = await provider.connect();
    const address =
      result?.publicKey?.toString?.() ||
      result?.account?.address ||

  }
  return null;
}

function libraryView() {
  const ownedCount = games.filter((game) => state.walletAddress && (game.owned || (game.id === "clockwork" && state.purchasedClockwork))).length;
  return `
    ${header("My Games")}
    <main class="library-shell">
      <section class="library-head">
        <div>
          <h1>My Games</h1>
          <p>Purchased games are always available here.</p>
        </div>
        <aside>
          <strong>${ownedCount} owned games</strong>
        <span>Last purchase: ${state.purchasedClockwork ? "Quiet Dungeon Treasure" : "Neon Nursery"}</span>
        </aside>
      </section>
      <section class="library-list">
        ${libraryRow(games[0])}
        ${libraryRow(games[3])}
        ${libraryRow(games[1])}
      </section>
      <section class="browse-demos">
        <p>Want more? Try demos in Discover.</p>
        <button class="secondary neutral" onclick="setView('detail')">${icon("play")} Browse demos</button>
      </section>
    </main>
  `;
}

function walletView() {
  return `
    ${header("Discover")}
    <main class="wallet-shell">
      <section class="wallet-card">
        <span class="wallet-large">${icon("wallet")}</span>
        <p class="eyeline cyan">Wallet login only</p>
        <h1>Connect wallet to continue</h1>
        <p class="copy">GameG uses MetaMask Solana wallet login as the only account system. Demo play stays open, but purchase, download, and My Games require a wallet.</p>
        <div class="button-stack">
          <button class="primary play" onclick="connectWallet()">${icon("wallet")} Connect MetaMask Solana</button>
          <button class="secondary neutral" onclick="connectLocalWallet()">${icon("wallet")} Local Test Wallet</button>
        </div>
        ${state.walletError ? `<p class="wallet-error">${state.walletError}</p>` : ""}
        <button class="text-link" onclick="setView('detail')">Back to Discover</button>
      </section>
    </main>
  `;
}

function render() {
  const views = {
    detail: detailView,
    demo: demoView,
    checkout: checkoutView,
    success: successView,
    library: libraryView,
    wallet: walletView,
  };
  app.innerHTML = views[state.view]();
}

render();

async function connectWallet() {
  state.walletError = "";
  try {
    const metaMaskWallet = await connectMetaMaskSolana();
    setConnectedWallet(metaMaskWallet.address, "MetaMask Solana", metaMaskWallet.wallet, metaMaskWallet.account);
    return;
  } catch (metaMaskError) {
    try {
      const injectedWallet = await connectInjectedSolanaWallet();
      if (injectedWallet) {
        setConnectedWallet(injectedWallet.address, injectedWallet.providerName, injectedWallet.provider, null);
        return;
      }
    } catch (injectedError) {
      state.walletError = injectedError && injectedError.message ? injectedError.message : "Solana wallet connection failed.";
      render();
      return;
    }
    const metaMaskHint = window.ethereum?.isMetaMask
      ? "MetaMask is detected, but its Solana account connector is unavailable. Enable Solana in MetaMask and try again."
      : "Install MetaMask, enable a Solana account, then try again.";
    state.walletError = `${metaMaskError.message || "MetaMask Solana connection failed."} ${metaMaskHint}`;
  }
  render();
}

async function handleApi(req, res, url) {
  if (req.method === "POST" && url.pathname === "/api/solana-rpc") {
    const payload = await body(req);
    const response = await fetch(rpcUrl, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    });
    const text = await response.text();
    res.writeHead(response.status, {
      "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
      "cache-control": "no-store",
    });
    res.end(text);
    return;
  }
  if (req.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/api/config") {
    json(res, 200, {
      gameId: config.gameId,
      gameTitle: config.gameTitle,
      amountUsdt: config.amountUsdt,
      tokenDecimals: config.tokenDecimals,
      tokenSymbol: config.tokenSymbol,
      chain: config.chain,
      recipient: config.recipient,
      usdtMint: config.usdtMint,
    });
    return;
  }
  if (req.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/api/wallet-readiness") {
    const walletAddress = url.searchParams.get("wallet");
    if (!walletAddress) {
      json(res, 400, { error: "wallet query parameter is required." });
      return;
    }
    const [solBalance, userTokenAccounts, receiverTokenAccounts] = await Promise.all([
      rpc("getBalance", [walletAddress, { commitment: "confirmed" }]),
      rpc("getTokenAccountsByOwner", [
        walletAddress,
        { mint: config.usdtMint },
        { encoding: "jsonParsed" },
      ]),
      rpc("getTokenAccountsByOwner", [
        config.recipient,
        { mint: config.usdtMint },
        { encoding: "jsonParsed" },
      ]),
    ]);
    const tokenAccount = userTokenAccounts.value?.[0] || null;
    const rawUsdt = BigInt(tokenAccount?.account?.data?.parsed?.info?.tokenAmount?.amount || "0");
    const hasReceiverTokenAccount = Boolean(receiverTokenAccounts.value?.length);
    const needs = [];
    if ((solBalance.value || 0) < 2_060_000) {
      needs.push("Add SOL to this wallet for mainnet fees and possible receiver USDT account creation.");
    }
    if (!tokenAccount) {
      needs.push("Add SPL USDT on Solana to this wallet.");
    } else if (rawUsdt < config.amountBaseUnits) {
      needs.push(`Add at least ${config.amountUsdt} USDT on Solana to this wallet.`);
    }
    json(res, 200, {
      walletAddress,
      solLamports: solBalance.value || 0,
      sol: (Number(solBalance.value || 0) / 1_000_000_000).toFixed(9),
      usdtBaseUnits: rawUsdt.toString(),
      usdt: (Number(rawUsdt) / 1_000_000).toFixed(6),
      hasUserUsdtTokenAccount: Boolean(tokenAccount),
      hasReceiverUsdtTokenAccount: hasReceiverTokenAccount,
      canPay: needs.length === 0,

const alphabet = "123456789ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijkmnopqrstuvwxyz";

function base58(bytes) {
  let value = BigInt(`0x${Buffer.from(bytes).toString("hex")}`);
  let encoded = "";
  while (value > 0n) {
    const mod = value % 58n;
    encoded = alphabet[Number(mod)] + encoded;
    value /= 58n;
  }
  for (const byte of bytes) {
    if (byte === 0) encoded = "1" + encoded;
    else break;
  }
  return encoded || "1";
}

function json(res, status, payload) {
  const data = JSON.stringify(payload);
  res.writeHead(status, {
    "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
    "cache-control": "no-store",
  });
  res.end(data);
}

async function body(req) {
  const chunks = [];
  for await (const chunk of req) chunks.push(chunk);
  if (!chunks.length) return {};
  return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8"));
}

function shortId() {
  return base58(randomBytes(9));
}

[16] user: <heartbeat>
  <automation_id>ai-ui</automation_id>
  <current_time_iso>2026-05-22T15:49:00.639Z</current_time_iso>
  <instructions>
围绕“AI 游戏平台：Demo 免费试玩，完整版付费下载；无链上资产；钱包仅作为可选登录/支付身份”的 Web 端产品，进行一轮极简且美观的 UI/功能设计优化。重点原则：平台功能必须简约，不做复杂游戏商店、开发者后台、链上资产、复杂下载管理或复杂筛选；UI 交互只围绕用户体验 Demo 和支付下载完整游戏。核心路径固定为：看到游戏 -> Play Demo -> 试玩 -> Buy Full Game -> 支付 -> Download。必须特别关注 UI 交互页面美观：试玩入口、购买按钮、支付状态、购买后 Download 状态、My Games 已购列表，都要有清晰层级、舒服留白、好看的游戏媒体展示、按钮状态一致、移动/桌面不拥挤。输出内容包括：1. 极简产品功能列表，只保留玩家端核心功能：游戏发现、免费试玩、登录、支付、下载、My Games 再次下载/更新；2. 用户使用流程，重点分析 Demo 试玩到付费购买的转化路径；3. 当前 UI 可删除/弱化的复杂功能，例如开发者分成面板、过多筛选项、复杂 checkout summary、钱包入口、下载管理复杂状态、平台后台感信息；4. 本轮 UI 优化方向：Play Demo 作为最强按钮，不登录也能试玩；Buy Full Game 固定可见并显示明确价格；支付成功后 Buy 变 Download；My Games 只显示已购游戏；交互页面要更美观、更清楚、更轻量；5. 使用 imagegen 输出该平台所有关键交互 UI 图，不只输出主页面。每轮至少生成这些界面图：A. 游戏发现/详情主页面；B. Demo 试玩页面；C. Buy Full Game 购买/支付页面；D. 支付成功后 Download 解锁页面；E. My Games 已购游戏/再次下载页面。所有图片都要求是实际产品界面，不是营销海报，视觉上要有高级感、留白、按钮层级和清晰交互状态，并保持同一设计系统。最终回复中展示全部生成的 UI 图片，并用极简中文总结本轮改动。
  </instructions>
</heartbeat>

async function sendWithWalletStandard(tx) {
  const feature = state.walletSigner?.features?.["solana:signAndSendTransaction"];
  if (!feature || typeof feature.signAndSendTransaction !== "function" || !state.walletAccount) {
    return "";
  }
  const txBytes = tx.serialize({ requireAllSignatures: false, verifySignatures: false });
  const result = await feature.signAndSendTransaction({
    account: state.walletAccount,
    chain: solanaChain,
    transaction: txBytes,
  });
  const signature = result?.signature || result?.signatures?.[0];
  return signature instanceof Uint8Array ? base58(signature) : signature || "";
}

async function sendWithInjectedWallet(tx, connection) {
  const provider = state.walletSigner;
  if (!provider) return "";
  if (typeof provider.signAndSendTransaction === "function") {
    const result = await provider.signAndSendTransaction(tx);
    return result?.signature?.toString?.() || result?.signature || "";
  }
  if (typeof provider.signTransaction === "function") {
    const signed = await provider.signTransaction(tx);
    const signature = await connection.sendRawTransaction(signed.serialize(), {
      skipPreflight: false,
      preflightCommitment: "confirmed",
    });
    return signature;
  }
  return "";
}

  );

    new web3.TransactionInstruction({

  );

  const latest = await connection.getLatestBlockhash("confirmed");
  tx.feePayer = payer;
  tx.recentBlockhash = latest.blockhash;
  return { tx, connection };
}

async function sendWithWalletStandard(tx) {
  const feature = state.walletSigner?.features?.["solana:signAndSendTransaction"];
  if (!feature || typeof feature.signAndSendTransaction !== "function" || !state.walletAccount) {
    return "";
  }
  const txBytes = tx.serialize({ requireAllSignatures: false, verifySignatures: false });
  const result = await feature.signAndSendTransaction({
    account: state.walletAccount,
    chain: solanaChain,
    transaction: txBytes,
  });
  const signature = result?.signature || result?.signatures?.[0];
  return signature instanceof Uint8Array ? base58(signature) : signature || "";
}

async function payWithWallet() {
  if (!state.walletAddress) {
    state.walletError = "Connect a Solana wallet before payment.";
    setView("wallet");
    return;
  }
  if (!state.walletSigner) {
    state.paymentError = "Reconnect MetaMask Solana before payment signing.";

  }
  if (!state.order) {
    const order = await createPaymentOrder();

  render();

    let signature = await sendWithWalletStandard(tx);
    if (!signature) signature = await sendWithInjectedWallet(tx, connection);
    if (!signature) throw new Error("Connected wallet does not support transaction signing.");
    state.order.signature = signature;
    state.paymentNotice = `Payment submitted: ${shortAddress(signature)}. Verifying on Solana...`;
    render();
    const paid = await verifyPaymentSignature(signature);
    if (paid) return;
  } catch (error) {
    state.paymentError = error && error.message ? error.message : "Wallet payment failed.";
  }
  state.paymentBusy = false;
  render();
}

async function connectMetaMaskSolana() {
  const module = await import(metaMaskSolanaSdkUrl);
  const createSolanaClient = module.createSolanaClient || module.default?.createSolanaClient;
  if (!createSolanaClient) {
    throw new Error("MetaMask Solana connector did not load.");
  }

  const client = await createSolanaClient({
    dapp: {
      name: brandName,
      url: window.location.href,
    },
    api: {
      supportedNetworks: {
        mainnet: solanaRpcUrl,
      },
    },
  });
  const wallet = typeof client.getWallet === "function" ? client.getWallet() : client.wallet;
  const connectFeature = wallet?.features?.["standard:connect"];
  if (!connectFeature || typeof connectFeature.connect !== "function") {
    throw new Error("MetaMask Solana account is not available.");
  }

  const result = await connectFeature.connect({ silent: false });
  const account = result?.accounts?.[0] || wallet.accounts?.[0];
  const address = account?.address || account?.publicKey?.toString();
  if (!address) {
    throw new Error("No Solana account returned from MetaMask.");
  }
  return { address, wallet, account };
}

async function connectInjectedSolanaWallet() {
  const providers = [
    window.solana,
    window.phantom?.solana,
    window.backpack?.solana,
    window.solflare,
  ].filter(Boolean);

  for (const provider of providers) {
    if (typeof provider.connect !== "function") continue;
    const result = await provider.connect();
    const address =
      result?.publicKey?.toString?.() ||
      result?.account?.address ||
      result?.accounts?.[0]?.address ||
      provider.publicKey?.toString?.();
    if (address) return { address, provider, providerName: provider.isPhantom ? "Phantom" : "Solana Wallet" };
  }
  return null;
}

async function handleApi(req, res, url) {
  if (req.method === "POST" && url.pathname === "/api/solana-rpc") {
    const payload = await body(req);
    const response = await fetch(rpcUrl, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    });
    const text = await response.text();
    res.writeHead(response.status, {
      "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
      "cache-control": "no-store",
    });
    res.end(text);
    return;
  }
  if (req.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/api/config") {
    json(res, 200, {
      gameId: config.gameId,
      gameTitle: config.gameTitle,
      amountUsdt: config.amountUsdt,
      tokenDecimals: config.tokenDecimals,
      tokenSymbol: config.tokenSymbol,
      chain: config.chain,
      recipient: config.recipient,
      usdtMint: config.usdtMint,
    });
    return;
  }
  if (req.method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/api/wallet-readiness") {
    const walletAddress = url.searchParams.get("wallet");
    if (!walletAddress) {
      json(res, 400, { error: "wallet query parameter is required." });
      return;
    }
    const [solBalance, userTokenAccounts, receiverTokenAccounts] = await Promise.all([
      rpc("getBalance", [walletAddress, { commitment: "confirmed" }]),
      rpc("getTokenAccountsByOwner", [
        walletAddress,
        { mint: config.usdtMint },
        { encoding: "jsonParsed" },
      ]),
      rpc("getTokenAccountsByOwner", [
        config.recipient,
        { mint: config.usdtMint },
        { encoding: "jsonParsed" },
      ]),
    ]);
    const tokenAccount = userTokenAccounts.value?.[0] || null;
    const rawUsdt = BigInt(tokenAccount?.account?.data?.parsed?.info?.tokenAmount?.amount || "0");
    const hasReceiverTokenAccount = Boolean(receiverTokenAccounts.value?.length);

      needs.push("Add SOL to this wallet for mainnet fees and possible receiver USDT account creation.");
    }
    if (!tokenAccount) {
      needs.push("Add SPL USDT on Solana to this wallet.");
    } else if (rawUsdt < config.amountBaseUnits) {
      needs.push(`Add at least ${config.amountUsdt} USDT on Solana to this wallet.`);

    json(res, 200, {

const alphabet = "123456789ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijkmnopqrstuvwxyz";

function base58(bytes) {
  let value = BigInt(`0x${Buffer.from(bytes).toString("hex")}`);
  let encoded = "";
  while (value > 0n) {
    const mod = value % 58n;
    encoded = alphabet[Number(mod)] + encoded;
    value /= 58n;
  }
  for (const byte of bytes) {
    if (byte === 0) encoded = "1" + encoded;
    else break;
  }
  return encoded || "1";
}

function json(res, status, payload) {
  const data = JSON.stringify(payload);
  res.writeHead(status, {
    "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",
    "cache-control": "no-store",
  });
  res.end(data);
}

async function body(req) {
  const chunks = [];
  for await (const chunk of req) chunks.push(chunk);
  if (!chunks.length) return {};
  return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8"));
}

function shortId() {
  return base58(randomBytes(9));

+      const text = await response.text();

+        "cache-control": "no-store",
+      });
+      res.end(text);
+    } catch (error) {
+      json(res, 504, { error: error.name === "AbortError" ? "Solana RPC timeout" : "Solana RPC failed" });
+    } finally {
+      clearTimeout(timeout);
+    }

async function pay() {

async function verifyPayment() {
  if (!state.order) return;
  state.paymentBusy = true;
  state.paymentError = "";
  state.paymentNotice = "";
  render();
  try {
    const payload = await api(`/api/orders/${state.order.id}/verify`, {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ signature: state.manualSignature.trim() || state.order.signature || "" }),
    });
    state.order = payload.order;
    if (payload.order.status === "paid") {
      setPaid(payload.order);
      return;
    }
    state.paymentError = payload.order.error || "Payment not confirmed yet.";
  } catch (error) {
    state.paymentError = error.message || "Payment verification failed.";
  }
  state.paymentBusy = false;
  render();
}

async function pay() {

async function verifyPayment() {
  if (!state.order) return;
  state.paymentBusy = true;
  state.paymentError = "";
  state.paymentNotice = "";
  render();
  try {
    const payload = await api(`/api/orders/${state.order.id}/verify`, {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ signature: state.manualSignature.trim() || state.order.signature || "" }),
    });
    state.order = payload.order;
    if (payload.order.status === "paid") {
      setPaid(payload.order);
      return;
    }
    state.paymentError = payload.order.error || "Payment not confirmed yet.";
  } catch (error) {
    state.paymentError = error.message || "Payment verification failed.";
  }
  state.paymentBusy = false;
  render();
}

                <div><span>USDT</span><strong>${state.walletReadiness.usdt}</strong></div>
                <div><span>User USDT ATA</span><strong>${state.walletReadiness.hasUserUsdtTokenAccount ? "Ready" : "Missing"}</strong></div>
                <div><span>Receiver USDT ATA</span><strong>${state.walletReadiness.hasReceiverUsdtTokenAccount ? "Ready" : "Will be created"}</strong></div>
              </div>`
            : ""
        }
        <button class="text-link" onclick="setView('demo')">Return to demo</button>
        <p class="secure">Only pay USDT on Solana. Do not send SOL, USDC, ETH, TRON USDT, or other tokens.</p>
        <div class="after-payment">${icon("download")} After payment: Download full build</div>
      </section>
    </main>
  `;
}

function setPaid(order) {
  state.order = order;
  state.purchasedClockwork = true;
  state.paymentError = "";
  localStorage.setItem("aigameDockPaidClockwork", "true");
  setView("success");
}

async function createPaymentOrder() {
  state.paymentBusy = true;
  state.paymentError = "";
  state.paymentNotice = "";
  render();
  try {
    const payload = await api("/api/orders", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ gameId: "clockwork", walletAddress: state.walletAddress }),
    });
    state.order = payload.order;
    state.paymentNotice = "Payment order created. Confirm the USDT transfer in your wallet.";
  } catch (error) {
    state.paymentError = error.message || "Could not create payment order.";
  }
  state.paymentBusy = false;
  render();
  return state.order;
}

async function checkWalletReadiness() {
  const payload = await api(`/api/wallet-readiness?wallet=${encodeURIComponent(state.walletAddress)}`, {
    headers: {},
  });
  state.walletReadiness = payload;
  if (!payload.canPay) {
    throw new Error(payload.needs.join(" "));
  }
  return payload;
}

async function copyPaymentAddress() {
  const value = state.order ? `${state.order.recipient}` : "7MsAkdANC9d9MxRz1G1nndz1F8TnHnWsZBFp2EVQiYE9";

}

async function copyManualPaymentDetails() {
  if (!state.order) await createPaymentOrder();
  if (!state.order) return;
  await navigator.clipboard.writeText(
    [
      "Network: Solana Mainnet",
      `Token: USDT (${state.order.usdtMint})`,
      `Recipient wallet: ${state.order.recipient}`,
      `Amount: ${state.order.amount} ${state.order.token}`,
      `Order memo: ${state.order.memo}`,
    ].join("\n"),
  );
  state.paymentNotice = "Payment details copied. Send Solana USDT from MetaMask, then paste the transaction signature here.";
  render();
}

function download() {
  if (!state.walletAddress) {
    state.view = "wallet";
    render();
    return;
  }
  state.downloading = true;
  render();
}

function shortAddress(address) {
  if (!address) return "";
  return `${address.slice(0, 6)}...${address.slice(-4)}`;
}

function libraryRow(game) {
  const isClockwork = game.id === "clockwork";
  const owned = state.walletAddress && (game.owned || (isClockwork && state.purchasedClockwork));
  if (!owned) return "";
  const action = game.installed ? "Update" : "Download";
  return `
    <article class="library-row ${game.tone}">
      <div class="cover">${isClockwork ? `<img src="${demoImageUrl}" alt="">` : ""}</div>
      <div class="library-main">
        <div>
          <h3>${game.title}</h3>
          <span class="owned">${game.installed ? "Installed" : "Owned"}</span>
        </div>
        <p>${game.platforms} · Version ${game.version} · ${game.size}</p>
        ${
          isClockwork && state.downloading
            ? `<div class="download-progress"><span>Downloading 42%</span><div class="bar"><i style="width:42%"></i></div><button>Cancel</button></div>`
            : ""
        }
      </div>
      <button class="primary download compact" onclick="${isClockwork ? "download()" : ""}">${icon(game.installed ? "download" : "download")} ${action}</button>
    </article>
  `;
}

function libraryView() {
  const ownedCount = games.filter((game) => state.walletAddress && (game.owned || (game.id === "clockwork" && state.purchasedClockwork))).length;
  return `

          <p>Purchased games are always available here.</p>

        <aside>

      <section class="browse-demos">
        <p>Want more? Try demos in Discover.</p>
        <button class="secondary neutral" onclick="setView('detail')">${icon("play")} Browse demos</button>
      </section>

function setPaid(order) {
  state.order = order;
  state.purchasedClockwork = true;
  state.paymentError = "";
  localStorage.setItem("aigameDockPaidClockwork", "true");
  setView("success");
}

async function createPaymentOrder() {
  state.paymentBusy = true;
  state.paymentError = "";
  state.paymentNotice = "";
  render();
  try {
    const payload = await api("/api/orders", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ gameId: "clockwork", walletAddress: state.walletAddress }),
    });
    state.order = payload.order;
    state.paymentNotice = "Payment order created. Confirm the USDT transfer in your wallet.";
  } catch (error) {
    state.paymentError = error.message || "Could not create payment order.";
  }
  state.paymentBusy = false;
  render();
  return state.order;
}

async function checkWalletReadiness() {
  const payload = await api(`/api/wallet-readiness?wallet=${encodeURIComponent(state.walletAddress)}`, {
    headers: {},
  });
  state.walletReadiness = payload;
  if (!payload.canPay) {
    throw new Error(payload.needs.join(" "));
  }
  return payload;
}

  if (!address) return "";
  return `${address.slice(0, 6)}...${address.slice(-4)}`;

function libraryRow(game) {
  const isClockwork = game.id === "clockwork";
  const owned = state.walletAddress && (game.owned || (isClockwork && state.purchasedClockwork));
  if (!owned) return "";
  const action = game.installed ? "Update" : "Download";
  return `
    <article class="library-row ${game.tone}">
      <div class="cover">${isClockwork ? `<img src="${demoImageUrl}" alt="">` : ""}</div>
      <div class="library-main">
        <div>
          <h3>${game.title}</h3>
          <span class="owned">${game.installed ? "Installed" : "Owned"}</span>
        </div>
        <p>${game.platforms} · Version ${game.version} · ${game.size}</p>
        ${
          isClockwork && state.downloading
            ? `<div class="download-progress"><span>Downloading 42%</span><div class="bar"><i style="width:42%"></i></div><button>Cancel</button></div>`
            : ""
        }
      </div>
      <button class="primary download compact" onclick="${isClockwork ? "download()" : ""}">${icon(game.installed ? "download" : "download")} ${action}</button>
    </article>
  `;
}

function libraryView() {
  const ownedCount = games.filter((game) => state.walletAddress && (game.owned || (game.id === "clockwork" && state.purchasedClockwork))).length;
  return `

# Browser

Use this skill for browser automation tasks such as inspecting pages, navigating, testing local apps, clicking, typing, taking screenshots, and reading visible page state. After setup, select the `iab` browser.

Keep browser work in the background by default.

Show the browser when the user's request is primarily to put a page in front of them or let them watch the interaction, such as "open localhost:3000", "go to the docs page", "take me to the PR", "show me the current tab", or "keep the browser open while you test checkout".

Do not show the browser when navigation is only a means to answer a question or verify behavior, such as "check localhost:3000 and tell me whether login works", "inspect the docs page and summarize what changed", or "verify the modal still opens correctly". Localhost targets and ordinary page navigation do not by themselves require visibility.

When the browser should be visible to the user, actually present it with `await (await browser.capabilities.get("visibility")).set(true)`.

If this plugin is listed as available in the session, treat that as mandatory reading before browser work. Open and follow this skill before saying that Browser is unavailable and before falling back to standalone Playwright or Computer Use.

Do not skip this skill just because Computer Use MCP tool calls are directly visible or appear easier to invoke. The presence of Computer Use tools is not evidence that Computer Use is the preferred browser surface.

Before using this skill for the first time in the current conversation context, read<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2463" />cell that never created the binding.

### General guidance

* Minimize interruptions as much as possible. Only ask clarifying questions if you really need to. If a user has an under-specified prompt, try to fulfill it first before asking for more information.
* Remember, the user is asking questions about what they see on the screen. Base your interactions on what is visible to the user (based on DOM and screenshots) rather than programmatically determining what they are talking about. The "first link" on the page is not necessarily the first `a href` in the DOM.
* Try not to over-complicate things. It is okay to click based on node ID if it is not clear how to determine the UI element in Playwright.
* If a tab is already on a given URL, do not call `goto` with the same URL. This will reload the page and may lose any in-progress information the user has provided. When you intentionally need to reload, call `tab.reload()`.
* If browser-use is interrupted because the extension or user took control, do not quote the raw runtime error. Summarize it naturally for the user, for example: "Browser use was stopped in the extension." Avoid internal terms like turn_id, runtime, retry, or plugin error text unless the user asks for details.
* When testing a user's local app on `localhost`, `127.0.0.1`, `::1`, or another local development URL in a framework that does not support hot reloading or hot reloading is disabled, call `tab.reload()` after code or build changes before verifying the UI. After reloading, take a fresh DOM snapshot or screenshot before continuing.
* Do not brute-force undocumented site search URLs, query parameter variants, search engine query grids, or candidate URL arrays unless the user explicitly asks for exhaustive coverage.
* If a guessed URL, search query, or candidate page fails, try at most one new approach. After that, switch to visible page navigation, the site's own search UI, or give the best current answer with uncertainty.

* If you use a search engine fallback, run one focused query, inspect the strongest results, and open the best candidate. Do not keep rewriting the query in loops.
* Once you have one strong candidate page, verify it directly instead of collecting more candidates.

### Screenshots
* If you take a screenshot that the user should see, include the image inline in your Markdown response using Markdown image syntax so the image renders, rather than as a bare link:
  ```md
  ![screenshot](IMAGE_LINK)

# Browser

Use this skill for browser automation tasks such as inspecting pages, navigating, testing local apps, clicking, typing, taking screenshots, and reading visible page state. After setup, select the `iab` browser.

Keep browser work in the background by default.

Show the browser when the user's request is primarily to put a page in front of them or let them watch the interaction, such as "open localhost:3000", "go to the docs page", "take me to the PR", "show me the current tab", or "keep the browser open while you test checkout".

Do not show the browser when navigation is only a means to answer a question or verify behavior, such as "check localhost:3000 and tell me whether login works", "inspect the docs page and summarize what changed", or "verify the modal still opens correctly". Localhost targets and ordinary page navigation do not by themselves require visibility.

When the browser should be visible to the user, actually present it with `await (await browser.capabilities.get("visibility")).set(true)`.

If this plugin is listed as available in the session, treat that as mandatory reading before browser work. Open and follow this skill before saying that Browser is unavailable and before falling back to standalone Playwright or Computer Use.

Do not skip this skill just because Computer Use MCP tool calls are directly visible or appear easier to invoke. The presence of Computer Use tools is not evidence that Computer Use is the preferred browser surface.

Before using this skill for the first time in the current conversation context, read<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2463" />cell that never created the binding.

### General guidance

* Minimize interruptions as much as possible. Only ask clarifying questions if you really need to. If a user has an under-specified prompt, try to fulfill it first before asking for more information.
* Remember, the user is asking questions about what they see on the screen. Base your interactions on what is visible to the user (based on DOM and screenshots) rather than programmatically determining what they are talking about. The "first link" on the page is not necessarily the first `a href` in the DOM.
* Try not to over-complicate things. It is okay to click based on node ID if it is not clear how to determine the UI element in Playwright.
* If a tab is already on a given URL, do not call `goto` with the same URL. This will reload the page and may lose any in-progress information the user has provided. When you intentionally need to reload, call `tab.reload()`.
* If browser-use is interrupted because the extension or user took control, do not quote the raw runtime error. Summarize it naturally for the user, for example: "Browser use was stopped in the extension." Avoid internal terms like turn_id, runtime, retry, or plugin error text unless the user asks for details.
* When testing a user's local app on `localhost`, `127.0.0.1`, `::1`, or another local development URL in a framework that does not support hot reloading or hot reloading is disabled, call `tab.reload()` after code or build changes before verifying the UI. After reloading, take a fresh DOM snapshot or screenshot before continuing.
* Do not brute-force undocumented site search URLs, query parameter variants, search engine query grids, or candidate URL arrays unless the user explicitly asks for exhaustive coverage.
* If a guessed URL, search query, or candidate page fails, try at most one new approach. After that, switch to visible page navigation, the site's own search UI, or give the best current answer with uncertainty.

* If you use a search engine fallback, run one focused query, inspect the strongest results, and open the best candidate. Do not keep rewriting the query in loops.
* Once you have one strong candidate page, verify it directly instead of collecting more candidates.
* When the page exposes one authoritative signal for the fact you need, such as a selected option, checked state, success modal or toast, basket line item, selected sort option, or current URL parameter, treat that as the answer unless another signal directly contradicts it.
* Do not keep re-verifying the same fact through header badges, alternate surfaces, or repeated full-page snapshots once an authoritative signal is already present.
